MS Sans Serif
Frame2
Start test as
MS Sans Serif
OPTIVO
Initial Valve Open
 Initial valve Open will be done as per data entry , Actual servo control starts after Preload cross
OPTAUTOSTRT
Pot. start
With Pot.  start , Valve will be operated from Panel Potentiometer till Preload, Actual servo control starts after Preload cross
OPTPOTSTRT
Auto Start
With Auto start , Initial valve Open will be selected from given Rate, Actual servo control starts after Preload cross
Label3
 * For Valve control up to Preload or 1% of full scale load (For Speciman Gripping)
Frame1
Test mode select
MS Sans Serif
OPTDISPHLD
Elongation Hold
OPTSTRAIN
Strain Rate Control
Testing will be done with Strain Rate control as per entered Strain rate , after Preload cross
OPTSTRESS
Stress Rate Control
Testing will be done with Stress Rate control as per entered Stress rate ,  after 1% Full scale load cross
OPTLDHLD
Load Hold
Testing will be done with Load Rate up to 75% of Load Hold & Hold Load will achieved & maintained for Hold Time in sec.
OPTELR
Elongation Rate control
